问题：U1700 的每个关键引脚分别连接到哪个网络？
参考答案：U1700 是 LP87702-Q1；VIN_B0_1、VIN_B1_1、VANA、VIN_B0_2、VIN_B1_2 接 VCC3V3，FB_B0 接 VCC1V1，FB_B1 接 VCC1V8，VOUT_BST 接 VCC50，EN1 接 MCU_5V_EN。
实际回答：根据证据，U1700 的所有引脚连接网络如下，未区分“关键引脚”与其它引脚，因证据未定义关键性列表。 **直接结论：** - nINT → MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1 - EN1 → MCU_5V_EN - VIN_B0_1 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_1 → VCC3V3 - VANA → VCC3V3 - SW_BST → N19935658 - NRST → N19935946 - SDA(EN3) → MCU_SDA_P15.5 - SCL(EN2) → MCU_SCL_P15.4 - FB_B1 → VCC1V8 - CLKIN(GPO2) → MCU_WD_INH - WDI → MCU_WDI_P13.3 - VOUT_BST → VCC50 - PG1(GPO1) → N19936014 - WD_RESET → TC367_RESET_N - SW_B1_1 → N19935668 - FB_B0 → VCC1V1 - SW_B0_1 → N19935443 - SW_B0_2 → N19935443 - VIN_B0_2 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_2 → VCC3V3 - SW_B1_2 → N19935668 - PAD → GND - AGND → GND - PGND_BST → GND - PGND_B1_2 → GND - PGND_B1_1 → GND - PGND_B0_2 → GND - PGND_B0_1 → GND **支撑证据：** - 证据 [1]（`circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1700`）提供了上述全部引脚与网络的对应关系。 - 证据 [2]（`circuit:21:instance:U1700`）确认 U1700 为器件 LP87702-Q1。 **缺失信息：** 证据未定义 U1700 的“关键引脚”列表，因此上述列表包含所有引脚。若用户需要特定分类（如电源、使能、复位、I2C 等），需额外说明。

问题：网络 VCC3V3 上连接了哪些主要芯片？
参考答案：VCC3V3 有 52 个连接；主要 IC 至少包括 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600。
实际回答：根据证据，网络 VCC3V3 直接连接的主要芯片（集成电路）有：U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800、U1802、U1804。 **支撑证据：** - 电路原理图数据（来源：[1] 825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）显示，网络 VCC3V3 直接连接了以下芯片的电源引脚： - U1500 (VIN) - U1600 (VCCIN) - U1700 (VIN_B0_1, VIN_B1_1, VANA, VIN_B0_2, VIN_B1_2) - U1800 (VIN, MODE/SYNC) - U1802 (VIN) - U1804 (VIN)
评分正文：根据证据，网络 VCC3V3 直接连接的主要芯片（集成电路）有：U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800、U1802、U1804。 **支撑证据：** - 电路原理图数据（来源：[1] 825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）显示，网络 VCC3V3 直接连接了以下芯片的电源引脚： - U1500 (VIN) - U1600 (VCCIN) - U1700 (VIN_B0_1, VIN_B1_1, VANA, VIN_B0_2, VIN_B1_2) - U1800 (VIN, MODE/SYNC) - U1802 (VIN) - U1804 (VIN)

Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. 问题：ADAS 项目的软硬件接口文档中，硬件架构包含哪些模块电路？
参考答案：HSI 2.3 节列出 KL30 电源管理、唤醒、电源、TC377 最小系统、EQ6 最小系统、摄像头最小系统、看门狗、温度采样、系统内电源电压采样、ETH、CAN/CAN FD 和 3V3 过压保护等电路。
实际回答：根据ADAS项目软硬件接口文档（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx），硬件架构包含以下模块电路： 1. KL30电源管理电路 2. 唤醒电路 3. 电源电路 4. TC377最小系统 5. EQ6最小系统 6. 摄像头最小系统 7. 看门狗电路 8. 温度采样电路 9. 系统内电源电压采样电源 10. ETH电路 11. CAN/CAN FD电路 12. 3V3过压保护电路 **来源说明**：文档第2.3节“模块电路 Module circuit”的目录（证据[2]）列出了上述12个模块电路，证据[1]和[5]也提供了部分模块名称的印证，但完整列表以目录为准。 未发现证据冲突，所有子问题均已覆盖，无需补充缺失信息。
评分正文：根据ADAS项目软硬件接口文档（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx），硬件架构包含以下模块电路： 1. KL30电源管理电路 2. 唤醒电路 3. 电源电路 4. TC377最小系统 5. EQ6最小系统 6. 摄像头最小系统 7. 看门狗电路 8. 温度采样电路 9. 系统内电源电压采样电源 10. ETH电路 11. CAN/CAN FD电路 12. 3V3过压保护电路 **来源说明**：文档第2.3节“模块电路 Module circuit”的目录（证据[2]）列出了上述12个模块电路，证据[1]和[5]也提供了部分模块名称的印证，但完整列表以目录为准。 未发现证据冲突，所有子问题均已覆盖，无需补充缺失信息。

The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system UBSW_ACQ_AN6信号是UBD经过100K 1%、10K 1%电阻分压后的模拟信号。 UBSW_ACQ_AN6 signal is an analog signal of UBD divided by 100K 1%、10K 1% resistors. Which is connected to the ADC pin of MCU for sampling. hw-v1-joint-main-pmicsuccess0.6480.3940.750not_applicable1.0000.5000.5001.000not_applicable
问题：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是哪颗？它输出哪些电压，软件如何配置？
参考答案：主 PMIC 是 U1700，即 LP87702-Q1，输入 VCC3V3，输出包括 VCC1V1、VCC1V8 和 VCC50；软件配置依据 HSI 7.2 节 LP87702 配置说明，通过 I²C 相关信号配置。
实际回答：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是 **LP87702**（或 LP87702-Q1）。它输出 **VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50** 三路电压。软件通过 **I2C 总线（MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5）** 配置其内部寄存器来设置输出电压和使能，并使用 **MCU 的 P15.3 引脚**使能 5V 输出。[证据16, 证据9, 证据13, 证据10, 证据14] **支撑结论所必需的最少证据：** 1. **主PMIC型号：LP87702** * 证据 [16] 明确指出：“VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。” * 证据 [9] 也提到：“VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) ... 现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。” * 证据 [13] 给出了该芯片的详细运行模式，并明确其型号为 LP87702-Q1。 2. **输出电压：VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50** * 证据 [16] 描述：“采用LP87702芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50”。 * 证据 [9] 同样描述该芯片方案会输出“VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50”。 3. **软件配置方法：通过I2C写寄存器，MCU引脚控制使能** * 证据 [16] 指出：“用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1输出电压和使能。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出”。 * 证据 [10] 在“LP87702配置”章节中说明：“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示”，并给出了表格引用。 * 证据 [14] 提供了具体的寄存器配置内容，例如“FS_I_OVUV_SAFE_REACTION1”寄存器用于设置过压/欠压反应。
评分正文：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是 **LP87702**（或 LP87702-Q1）。它输出 **VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50** 三路电压。软件通过 **I2C 总线（MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5）** 配置其内部寄存器来设置输出电压和使能，并使用 **MCU 的 P15.3 引脚**使能 5V 输出。[证据16, 证据9, 证据13, 证据10, 证据14] **支撑结论所必需的最少证据：** 1. **主PMIC型号：LP87702** * 证据 [16] 明确指出：“VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。” * 证据 [9] 也提到：“VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) ... 现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。” * 证据 [13] 给出了该芯片的详细运行模式，并明确其型号为 LP87702-Q1。 2. **输出电压：VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50** * 证据 [16] 描述：“采用LP87702芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50”。 * 证据 [9] 同样描述该芯片方案会输出“VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50”。 3. **软件配置方法：通过I2C写寄存器，MCU引脚控制使能** * 证据 [16] 指出：“用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1输出电压和使能。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出”。 * 证据 [10] 在“LP87702配置”章节中说明：“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示”，并给出了表格引用。 * 证据 [14] 提供了具体的寄存器配置内容，例如“FS_I_OVUV_SAFE_REACTION1”寄存器用于设置过压/欠压反应。

Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | ADC电压采集阈值 The ADC voltage acquisition thresholdWCCA计算结果如下，软件应按照如下过欠压阈值设置：The WCCA calculation results are as follows. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F05</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>Camera实现图像采集功能，根据ADAS系统需求，选择图像传感器芯片IMX-728作为camera主芯片。 The Camera realizes image acquisition function. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. 问题：0.75V 电源由哪颗芯片产生，输入是什么，文档有什么配置要求？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 接 VCC3V3，VOSNS 对应 VCC0V75；配置要求见 HSI 7.3 节 TPS62872 配置。
实际回答：0.75V电源由TI的电源芯片**TPS62872**产生，其输入为**VCC3V3 (3.3V)**。文档中对该芯片的配置要求包括：通过MCU的P22.0引脚使能，通过I2C通信（MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4）设置输出电压和通信，I2C地址为0x41，用于EQ6内核供电。 ### 1. 产生0.75V电源的芯片型号 - **结论**：芯片型号为TI的**TPS62872**。 - **支撑证据**： - 文档[5]明确指出：“VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的方案TPS62872。” - 文档[6]同样确认：“VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。” - 文档[18]的表格中列出：“F14 POWER: TPS62872，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC0V75电压输出，EQ6L核电供电。” ### 2. 芯片的输入电源来源 - **结论**：输入电源为**VCC3V3**（3.3V）。 - **支撑证据**： - 文档[5]描述：“VCC3V3 to VCCOV75 is applied to the kernel power supply of EQ6...TI's scheme TPS62872 is selected.”，明确说明输入为VCC3V3。 - 文档[6]描述：“VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6...”，同样确认输入为VCC3V3。 - 文档[18]的表格中列出：“F14 POWER: TPS62872，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC0V75电压输出。” ### 3. 文档中的配置要求 - **结论**：配置要求包括：使能控制、I2C通信设置输出电压、电压范围与输出配置。 - **支撑证据**： - **使能与I2C通信**：文档[5]描述：“用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。” 文档[6]有相同描述。文档[19]提供I2C地址：“TPS62872地址：0x41”。 - **输出电压设置**：文档[9]（数据手册）说明输出电压通过VSET寄存器设置，有四个电压范围（如0.4V-0.71875V等），输出计算公式为 `Output voltage setpoint = 0.4 V + VSET[7:0] × 1.25 mV`（Range 1）。文档[13]进一步说明通过VRANGE[1:0]和控制寄存器设置范围，并通过VSET[7:0]位设置具体电压。 - **默认输出与启动**：文档[20]显示通过VSEL引脚可设置默认输出电压（如850mV、800mV等），但实际输出0.75V需通过I2C配置。文档[10]说明软启动时间可配置（默认1ms），且启动期间忽略新电压值直到序列完成。 **缺失信息**：未提供TPS62872芯片的详细外部元件配置（如电感、电容值），但文档[17]给出了输入电压范围（2.7V-6V）和电感范围（110nH-330nH，特定条件下55nH-330nH）。此信息为数据手册通用规格，非本项目特定配置。
评分正文：0.75V电源由TI的电源芯片**TPS62872**产生，其输入为**VCC3V3 (3.3V)**。文档中对该芯片的配置要求包括：通过MCU的P22.0引脚使能，通过I2C通信（MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4）设置输出电压和通信，I2C地址为0x41，用于EQ6内核供电。 ### 1. 产生0.75V电源的芯片型号 - **结论**：芯片型号为TI的**TPS62872**。 - **支撑证据**： - 文档[5]明确指出：“VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的方案TPS62872。” - 文档[6]同样确认：“VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。” - 文档[18]的表格中列出：“F14 POWER: TPS62872，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC0V75电压输出，EQ6L核电供电。” ### 2. 芯片的输入电源来源 - **结论**：输入电源为**VCC3V3**（3.3V）。 - **支撑证据**： - 文档[5]描述：“VCC3V3 to VCCOV75 is applied to the kernel power supply of EQ6...TI's scheme TPS62872 is selected.”，明确说明输入为VCC3V3。 - 文档[6]描述：“VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6...”，同样确认输入为VCC3V3。 - 文档[18]的表格中列出：“F14 POWER: TPS62872，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC0V75电压输出。” ### 3. 文档中的配置要求 - **结论**：配置要求包括：使能控制、I2C通信设置输出电压、电压范围与输出配置。 - **支撑证据**： - **使能与I2C通信**：文档[5]描述：“用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。” 文档[6]有相同描述。文档[19]提供I2C地址：“TPS62872地址：0x41”。 - **输出电压设置**：文档[9]（数据手册）说明输出电压通过VSET寄存器设置，有四个电压范围（如0.4V-0.71875V等），输出计算公式为 `Output voltage setpoint = 0.4 V + VSET[7:0] × 1.25 mV`（Range 1）。文档[13]进一步说明通过VRANGE[1:0]和控制寄存器设置范围，并通过VSET[7:0]位设置具体电压。 - **默认输出与启动**：文档[20]显示通过VSEL引脚可设置默认输出电压（如850mV、800mV等），但实际输出0.75V需通过I2C配置。文档[10]说明软启动时间可配置（默认1ms），且启动期间忽略新电压值直到序列完成。 **缺失信息**：未提供TPS62872芯片的详细外部元件配置（如电感、电容值），但文档[17]给出了输入电压范围（2.7V-6V）和电感范围（110nH-330nH，特定条件下55nH-330nH）。此信息为数据手册通用规格，非本项目特定配置。

None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F05</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>上电模式、运行模式、复位模式、下电模式 Power on mode, Power off mode, Run mode,Reset mode</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>1、上电模式 MCU使能EQ6系统电源，EQ6上电后将VCC3V3_EQ和VCC1V8通过FPC连接器提供给摄像头，其中VCC3V3_EQ再转换成VCC1V1，EQ6运行后配置RESET_N为高电平，并通过I2C配置CAM参数，摄像头开始工作。 2、运行模式 摄像头正常运行，输出图像信息给EQ6。 3、复位模式 EQ6配置摄像头的RESET_N信号为低电平，CAM进入复位模式。 4、下电模式 MCU控制EQ6系统下电（VCC3V3_EQ、VCC1V8）。 Power on mode MCU enables EQ6 system power supply, After EQ6 is powered on, VCC3V3_EQ and VCCIV8 are provided to the camera through the FPC connector, where VCC3V3_EQ is converted into VCC1V1. Run mode The camera operates normally and outputs the image information to EQ6. Reset mode EQ6 config the RESET_N signal of the camera to be low and the CAM enters the reset mode. Detection of the state of the FSEL pin works as follows: To detect the most significant bit (MSB), the circuit opens S1 and S2, and the input buffer detects if a high or a low level is connected to the FSEL pin. If the input buffer detects a high level, LSB = 1. If the circuit detects a low level, LSB = 0. If the MSB was 1, the circuit closes S2. If the input buffer detects a low level, LSB = 0. If the circuit detects a· | TPS6287x-Q1 2.7-V to 6-V Input, 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, 15-A, Stackable, Synchronous Step- Down Converters with Fast Transient Response 3 Description 1 Features AEC-Q100 qualified for automotive applications The TPS6287x-Q1 is a family of pin-to-pin 6-A, 9- Temperature grade 1: -40°C to +125°C, TA A, 12-A, and 15-A synchronous step-down DC/DC converters with differential remote sensing. For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation | 9 Detailed Description 9.1overview The TPS6287x-Q1 devices are synchronous step-down (buck) DC/DC converters. These devices use an enhanced DCS control topology to achieve fast transient response while switching with a fixed frequency. Together, with their low output voltage ripple, high DC accuracy, and differential remote sensing, these devices are ideal for supplying the cores of modern high-performance processors. The family of devices includes 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, and 15-A devices. To further increase the output current capability, the user can combine multiple devices in a “stack". For example, a stack of two TPS62873-Q1 devices have a current capability of 30 A. Each device of a stack must have the same current rating to avoid that one device enters current limit too early. For each current rating, there are full-featured devices with an I2C interface and limited-featured devices without an I2C interface (see the Device Options). The user can use a device variant without I2C in exactly the same way as a device variant with I?C, except that: · The user must connect the unused SCL and SDA pins to GND · The user must be aware of the (fixed) factory settings for parameters and functions that are programmable in the I²C device variants. 9.2 Functional Block Diagram 9.3FeatureDescription 9.3.1 Fixed-Frequency DCS Control Topology TPS6287x-Q1 devices. This topology is comprised of an inner emulated current loop, a middle direct feedback loop, and an outer voltage-regulating loop. 9.3.2 Forced PwM and Power Save Modes The device can control the inductor current in three different ways to regulate the output: Pulse-width modulation with continuous inductor current (PWM-CCM) Pulse-width modulation with discontinuous inductor current (PWM-DCM) Pulse-frequency modulation with discontinuous inductor current and pulse skipping (PFM-DCM) During PWM-CCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is continuous (see Figure 9-2). PwM operation achieves the lowest output voltage ripple and the best transient performance. During PWM-DCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is discontinuous (see Figure 9-3). In this mode, the device controls the peak inductor current to maintain the selected switching frequency while still being able to regulate the output. | high level, LSB = 1. 9.3.6 Output Voltage Setting 9.3.6.1 Output Voltage Range The device has four different voltage ranges. The VRANGE[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register control which range is active (see Table 9-3). The default output voltage range after device initialization is 0.4 V to 1.675 V in 5-mV steps. Note that every change to the VRANGE[1:0] bits must be followed by a write to the VSET register, even if the value of the VSET[7:o] bits does not change. This sequence is required for the device to start to use the new voltage range. Also note that the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range uses a 0.8-V reference and the other ranges use a 0.4-V reference. Switching to and from the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range can therefore cause increased output voltage undershoot or overshoot while the device switches its internal reference. In device variants that do not have I?C, the output voltage range is factory-set to 0.4 V to 1.675 V. 9.3.6.2OutputVoltageSetpoint Together with the selected range, the VSET[7:0] bits in the VSET register control the output voltage setpoint of the device (see Table 9-4). During initialization, the device reads the state of the VSEL pin and selects the default output voltage according to Table 9-5. Note that the VSEL pin also selects the I?C target address of the device (see Table 9-10). If the user programs new output voltage setpoint (VOUT[7:0]), output voltage range (VRANGE[1:0]), or soft-start time (SSTIME[1:o]) settings when the device has already begun its soft-start sequence, the device ignores the new values until the soft-start sequence is complete. If the user changes the value of VSET[7:0] during soft start, the device first ramps to the value that VSET[7:0] had when the soft-start sequence began. Then, when soft start is complete, ramps up or down to the new value. If the user changes VOUT[7:0], VRAMP[1:0], or SSTIME[1:0] while EN is low, the device uses the new values the next time the user enables it. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F27</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>上电模式、下电模式、运行模式 Power on mode, power off mode, run mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>1、 上电模式 一级电源VCC3V3_MCU输出有效后，MCU的内置驱动器与外置的MOS/电感/电容实现3V3_MCU转1V25_MCU的BUCK电源。 2、 运行模式 MCU的内置驱动器与外置的MOS/电感/电容构成的BUCK电源正常输出VCC1V25_MCU，用于MCU核供电。 3、 下电模式 一级电源VCC3V3_MCU输出掉电后，MCU的内置驱动器开始关断VCC1V25_MCU。 1.Power on mode After the output of VCC3V3_MCU is effective, the built-in driver of MCU and the external MOS/ inductor/capacitor realize the BUCK power from 3V3 MCU to 1V25 MCU. 2.Run mode MCU built-in driver and external MOS/ inductor/capacitor BUCK power supply composed of normal output VCC1V25 MCU for MCU core power supply. 3.Power off mode After the output power of the VCC3V3_MCU fails, the built-in driver of the MCU starts to shut down the VCC1V25 MCU.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td> </td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F32</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>3V3转1V25_MCU用于MCU的核供电 评分上下文：7/27 段，12000/40990 字符；已裁剪：是

问题：3.3V 电源有没有过压保护，是如何实现的？
参考答案：HSI 2.3.12 节声明存在 3V3 过压保护电路；EDF 只能用于说明观察到的二极管或 TVS 连接拓扑，不能在没有数据手册证据时断言具体阈值或保护能力。
实际回答：**结论：3.3V电源（VCC3V3_MCU）有外部过压保护电路。** **1. 是否存在过压保护？** - **是**。设计文档中明确包含一个名为“overvoltage protect”（模块编号F18）的模块，其功能定义为“用于MCU电源VCC3V3_MCU的外部过压保护”[2]。 **2. 如何实现？** 该过压保护电路通过以下方式实现： - **触发阈值**：当VCC3V3电压超过 **4.03V** 时，保护电路被触发[7]。 - **动作机制**：电路通过一个NMOS和PMOS组成的开关路径控制VCC3V3的输出。当VCC3V3 > 4.03V时，保护电路会使NMOS和PMOS关闭，从而切断VCC3V3_SW（即受保护的后级负载的供电），实现过压保护[7]。 - **正常导通**：当VCC3V3电压介于NMOS的VGS阈值与4.03V之间时，NMOS和PMOS导通，VCC3V3直通至后级[7]。 **缺失信息：** - **具体器件型号**：该保护电路使用的具体器件（如NMOS、PMOS、电压检测芯片的型号）在提供的证据中未明确列出。 - **电路原理图连接**：提供的电路设计数据（.EDF文件）仅显示了VCC3V3_MCU网络连接的元件列表[14]及各模块间的GND、I2C等网络连接[15][16][17][18][19][20]，但未包含该过压保护模块（F18）内部的详细原理图连接关系，因此无法确认该电路在原理图中的具体实现细节。
评分正文：**结论：3.3V电源（VCC3V3_MCU）有外部过压保护电路。** **1. 是否存在过压保护？** - **是**。设计文档中明确包含一个名为“overvoltage protect”（模块编号F18）的模块，其功能定义为“用于MCU电源VCC3V3_MCU的外部过压保护”[2]。 **2. 如何实现？** 该过压保护电路通过以下方式实现： - **触发阈值**：当VCC3V3电压超过 **4.03V** 时，保护电路被触发[7]。 - **动作机制**：电路通过一个NMOS和PMOS组成的开关路径控制VCC3V3的输出。当VCC3V3 > 4.03V时，保护电路会使NMOS和PMOS关闭，从而切断VCC3V3_SW（即受保护的后级负载的供电），实现过压保护[7]。 - **正常导通**：当VCC3V3电压介于NMOS的VGS阈值与4.03V之间时，NMOS和PMOS导通，VCC3V3直通至后级[7]。 **缺失信息：** - **具体器件型号**：该保护电路使用的具体器件（如NMOS、PMOS、电压检测芯片的型号）在提供的证据中未明确列出。 - **电路原理图连接**：提供的电路设计数据（.EDF文件）仅显示了VCC3V3_MCU网络连接的元件列表[14]及各模块间的GND、I2C等网络连接[15][16][17][18][19][20]，但未包含该过压保护模块（F18）内部的详细原理图连接关系，因此无法确认该电路在原理图中的具体实现细节。

After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. Definition of Operation mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN NRST电压低于阈值。LP87702-Q1器件停止工作。 READ OTP 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO电平，并且NRST电压高于阈值电平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平和偏置电路被启用。OTP配置被加载到寄存器。 STANDBY 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平、控制和偏压电路被启用。所有的寄存器都可以被主机处理器通过系统串口读写。电压转换器可以根据需要进行使能输出。 ACTIVE 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。稳压DC/DC变换器处于使能状态。所有的寄存器都可以被MCU通过系统串口读写。 SHUTDOWN After the enable signal MCU_OV75_MIPI_DDR_EN=L, the secondary power stops the 0V75_MIPI_DDR power supply. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电防反、保护和滤波。 An EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply anti-reaction, protection and filtering.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护，UBD供电口增加肖特基二极管，用于防反接保护，后级增加Π型滤波电路。 The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors to the UBD network, the PCB layout is near the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, the bidirectional TVS tube is added to the back end for transient overvoltage protection, the Schottky diode is added to the UBD power supply port for anti-reverse connection protection, and the type 1 filter circuit is added to the rear stage. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F18</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>AOU要求MCU需要硬件外部增加过压保护电路对3V3_MCU进行电压监控。 AOU requires MCU to increase the overvoltage protection circuit outside the hardware to monitor the voltage of 3V3_MCU. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td> </td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>1.当VCC3V3 <4.03V时，cathode引脚为高阻态, 当VCC3V3 <VGS门限（Q1701）时，NMOS关闭，PMOS关闭，VCC3V3_SW无电。 2、当4.03V＞VCC3V3 > VGS门限（Q1701）时，NMOS打开，PMOS打开，VCC3V3_SW和VCC3V3直通。 3、当VCC3V3＞4.03V时，cathode引脚输出逻辑低（最高1V），NMOS关闭，PMOS关闭，VCC3V3_SW无电，对后级负载起到过压保护作用。 1. When VCC3V3 <4.03V, the cathode pin is in the high resistance state. through. 3.When VCC3V3>4.03V, the cathode pin output logic is low (up to 1V), NMOS is off, PMOS is off, and VCC3V3_Sw has no power, which plays an overvoltage protection role on the load of the later 问题：板上用 TPS22918 做了哪些电源开关？逐个给出输入、输出和使能。
参考答案：U1500：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_ETH，使能 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN；U1802：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_EQ，使能 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN；U1803：VCC1V8 到 VCC1V8_EQ，使能 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：板上使用 TPS22918 共做了三个电源开关，分别以 U1500、U1802、U1803 为实例。每个开关的输入 (VIN)、输出 (VOUT) 和使能 (ON) 引脚连接如下： | 实例 | 输入 (VIN) | 输出 (VOUT) | 使能 (ON) | |------|------------|-------------|-----------| | U1500 | VCC3V3 | VCC3V3_ETH | MCU_3V3_ETH_EN | | U1802 | VCC3V3 | VCC3V3_EQ | MCU_3V3_EQ_EN | | U1803 | VCC1V8 | VCC1V8_EQ | MCU_1V8_EQ_EN | **支撑证据** - U1500 的引脚映射：VIN → VCC3V3，VOUT → VCC3V3_ETH，ON → MCU_3V3_ETH_EN（证据 [2]）。 - U1802 的引脚映射：VIN → VCC3V3，VOUT → VCC3V3_EQ，ON → MCU_3V3_EQ_EN（证据 [1]）。 - U1803 的引脚映射：VIN → VCC1V8，VOUT → VCC1V8_EQ，ON → MCU_1V8_EQ_EN（证据 [3]）。 - 三个实例均标注为 TPS22918TDBVRQ1（证据 [12][13][14]）。 **缺失信息**：无。所有证据覆盖完整，无冲突。
评分正文：板上使用 TPS22918 共做了三个电源开关，分别以 U1500、U1802、U1803 为实例。每个开关的输入 (VIN)、输出 (VOUT) 和使能 (ON) 引脚连接如下： | 实例 | 输入 (VIN) | 输出 (VOUT) | 使能 (ON) | |------|------------|-------------|-----------| | U1500 | VCC3V3 | VCC3V3_ETH | MCU_3V3_ETH_EN | | U1802 | VCC3V3 | VCC3V3_EQ | MCU_3V3_EQ_EN | | U1803 | VCC1V8 | VCC1V8_EQ | MCU_1V8_EQ_EN | **支撑证据** - U1500 的引脚映射：VIN → VCC3V3，VOUT → VCC3V3_ETH，ON → MCU_3V3_ETH_EN（证据 [2]）。 - U1802 的引脚映射：VIN → VCC3V3，VOUT → VCC3V3_EQ，ON → MCU_3V3_EQ_EN（证据 [1]）。 - U1803 的引脚映射：VIN → VCC1V8，VOUT → VCC1V8_EQ，ON → MCU_1V8_EQ_EN（证据 [3]）。 - 三个实例均标注为 TPS22918TDBVRQ1（证据 [12][13][14]）。 **缺失信息**：无。所有证据覆盖完整，无冲突。

问题：以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来？
参考答案：供电链路为 VCC3V3 → U1500（TPS22918，ON=MCU_3V3_ETH_EN）→ VCC3V3_ETH → U1501（TPS74501 LDO）→ VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：根据现有证据，无法完整回答“以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来”。以下按子问题分别说明。 --- **子问题 1：以太网PHY芯片的1.0V电源轨在电源树或设计文档中的名称是什么？** **结论：** 未明确标识。 - 支撑证据：电路图[1]显示以太网PHY芯片U1400（DP83TC812R-Q1）的电源引脚包括 VDDA、VDDIO、VDDMAC、VSLEEP，但这些引脚连接的网络（N22956977、N22956687、VSLEEP）均未标明电压值，设计文档也未提及1.0V电源轨的具体名称。 - **缺口：** 缺少明确标注为“1.0V”或对应电压值的电源网络名称。 --- **子问题 2：该电源轨的输入来源（上一级电源）是什么？使用了哪种转换器（LDO/DC-DC）？输入电压和转换参数是多少？** **结论：** 该信息完全缺失。 - 无任何直接支撑证据（电路图中未发现从该电源轨到上游转换器的连接，电源树文档中无相关描述，数据手册也未给出供电链路）。 - **缺口：** 缺少上一级电源节点、转换器类型（LDO/DC-DC）、输入电压及转换参数。 --- **子问题 3：上一级电源的输入来源又是什么？是否需要继续追溯直到系统输入电源？** **结论：** 无法追溯。 - 尽管覆盖度报告标记该子问题为“covered”，但实际支撑证据（如[19]描述的VCC3V3转VCC3V3_ETH、VCC3V3转VCC0V75等）均未涉及1.0V电源链，与以太网PHY的1.0V电源无关。 - **缺口：** 由于子问题2完全缺失，本子问题无有效证据支撑，无法给出任何一级的电源来源。 --- **总结：** 现有证据中，以太网PHY的1.0V电源轨名称、供电链路（从哪一级电源、通过何种转换器过来）均未找到直接说明。若要回答问题，需补充电源树文档、电路图或系统设计说明中关于该1.0V电源的完整供电路径。
评分正文：根据现有证据，无法完整回答“以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来”。以下按子问题分别说明。 --- **子问题 1：以太网PHY芯片的1.0V电源轨在电源树或设计文档中的名称是什么？** **结论：** 未明确标识。 - 支撑证据：电路图[1]显示以太网PHY芯片U1400（DP83TC812R-Q1）的电源引脚包括 VDDA、VDDIO、VDDMAC、VSLEEP，但这些引脚连接的网络（N22956977、N22956687、VSLEEP）均未标明电压值，设计文档也未提及1.0V电源轨的具体名称。 - **缺口：** 缺少明确标注为“1.0V”或对应电压值的电源网络名称。 --- **子问题 2：该电源轨的输入来源（上一级电源）是什么？使用了哪种转换器（LDO/DC-DC）？输入电压和转换参数是多少？** **结论：** 该信息完全缺失。 - 无任何直接支撑证据（电路图中未发现从该电源轨到上游转换器的连接，电源树文档中无相关描述，数据手册也未给出供电链路）。 - **缺口：** 缺少上一级电源节点、转换器类型（LDO/DC-DC）、输入电压及转换参数。 --- **子问题 3：上一级电源的输入来源又是什么？是否需要继续追溯直到系统输入电源？** **结论：** 无法追溯。 - 尽管覆盖度报告标记该子问题为“covered”，但实际支撑证据（如[19]描述的VCC3V3转VCC3V3_ETH、VCC3V3转VCC0V75等）均未涉及1.0V电源链，与以太网PHY的1.0V电源无关。 - **缺口：** 由于子问题2完全缺失，本子问题无有效证据支撑，无法给出任何一级的电源来源。 --- **总结：** 现有证据中，以太网PHY的1.0V电源轨名称、供电链路（从哪一级电源、通过何种转换器过来）均未找到直接说明。若要回答问题，需补充电源树文档、电路图或系统设计说明中关于该1.0V电源的完整供电路径。

None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F24</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>正常模式、复位模式，待机模式，断电模式，休眠模式 Normal mode, reset mode, standby mode, power off mode, sleep mode</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>1、 正常模式 模块上电，正常运行模式，以太网收发正常。MDIO可以访问，Link处于连接状态。 2、 复位模式 UV或Reset event时，PHY芯片将进行复位，在Reset期间将无法LINKUP，MDIO无法总线访问 待机模式 仅当PHY为MASTER且配置成管理模式时，上电自动进入待机模式。如果是自动模式，上电后自动进入正常模式。 4、 断电模式 当任何一个电源轨低于POR阈值（0.6V）时，PHY处于断电状态，所有数字IO都将保持高阻态。 休眠模式 当有休眠指令且MDI总线空闲时，将进入休眠模式，除了能量检测外，禁用所有PHY模块。在睡眠模式下，所有寄存器配置都会丢失。 Normal mode Power on，Normal Run mode, Ethernet transceiver is normal. MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Note that all register configurations will be lost during sleep mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | 10.4PowerSupplyRecommendations The TPS6287x-Q1 family has no special requirements for its input power supply. The output current rating of the input power supply must be rated according to the supply voltage and current requirements of the TPS6287X-Q1 10.5Layout 10.5.1 Layout Guidelines Achieving the performance the TPS6287x-Q1 devices are capable of requires proper PDN and PCB design. TI therefore recommends the user perform a power integrity analysis on their design. There are a number of commercially available power integrity software tools, and the user can use these tools to model the effects on performance of the PCB layout and passive components. In addition to the use of power integrity tools, Tl recommends the following basic principles: · Place the input capacitors close to the VIN and GND pins. Position the input capacitors in order of increasing size, starting with the smallest capacitors closest to the VIN and GND pins. Use an identical layout for both VIN-GND pin pairs of the package, to gain maximum benefit from the butterfly configuration. ·Place the inductor close to the device and keep the SW node small. · Connect the exposed thermal pad and the GND pins of the device together. Use multiple thermal vias to connect the exposed thermal pad of the device to one or more ground planes (Tl's EVM uses nine 150-μm thermal vias). ·Use multiple power and ground planes. · Route the VOSNS and GOSNS remote sense lines as a differential pair and connect them to the lowest- impedance point of the PDN. If the desired connection point is not the lowest impedance point of the PDN, optimize the PDN until it is. Do not route the VOSNS and GOSNS close to any of the switch nodes. · Connect the compensation components between VOSNS and GOSNS. Do not connect the compensation components directly to power ground. · If possible, distribute the output capacitors evenly between the TPS6287x-Q1 device and the point-of-load, rather than placing them altogether in one place. · Use multiple vias to connect each capacitor pad to the power and ground planes (TI's EVM typically uses four vias per pad). Use plenty of stitching vias to ensure a low impedance connection between different power and ground· planes. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | 表3.2 系统功能矩阵表运行模式定义 Definition of Operation Mode系统运行模式描述系统硬件启动流程，硬件初始化到软件接管控制的流程介绍。主要包括上电运行流程、下电流程。System operation mode describes the system hardware start-up process, hardware initialization to software take over control process. In POR mode, no functions are available and the content of the device registers is not valid. The device leaves POR mode and enters UVLO mode when the supply voltage increases above the POR threshold 9.4.2 Undervoltage Lockout The device operates in UVLO mode when the supply voltage is between the POR and UVLO thresholds. If the device enters UVLO mode from POR mode, no functions are available. If the device enters UVLO mode from standby mode, the output discharge function is available. The content of the device registers is valid in UVLO mode. The device leaves UVLO mode and enters POR mode when the supply voltage decreases below the POR threshold. The device leaves UVLO mode and enters standby mode when the supply voltage increases above the UVLO threshold. 9.4.3 Standby The device operates in standby mode when the supply voltage is greater than the UVLO threshold (and the device has completed its initialization) and any of the following conditions is true: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. SWEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register.· The device junction temperature is greater than the thermal shutdown threshold. ·The supply voltage is greater than the OVLO threshold. The device initializes for 4o0 μs (typical) after the supply voltage increases above the UVLO threshold voltage following a device power-on reset. If the supply voltage decreases below the UVLO threshold but not below the POR threshold, the device does not reinitialize when the supply voltage increases again. During initialization, the device reads the state of the FSEL, VSEL, and SYNC_OUT pins. The following functions are available in standby mode: · I2C interface ·Output discharge ·Power good Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 The device leaves standby mode and enters UVLO mode when the supply voltage decreases below the UVLO threshold. The device leaves standby mode and enters on mode when all of the following conditions are true: · A high-level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The device junction temperature is below the thermal shutdown threshold.· · The supply voltage is below the OVLO threshold.

问题：主控 MCU 是哪颗，型号是什么？
参考答案：EDF 中 U900 为 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，即 AURIX TC367；HSI 章节名称写作 TC377 最小系统。回答应披露 TC367 与 TC377 的来源差异，不能合并为无冲突的单一型号。
实际回答：主控MCU的型号是 **SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA**。 **支撑证据**： - 文档[10]（来源：600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）中明确说明：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片”。 - 文档[15]（同上来源）的模块功能定义表中，F01模块“MCU”的功能定义为“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”。
评分正文：主控MCU的型号是 **SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA**。 **支撑证据**： - 文档[10]（来源：600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）中明确说明：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片”。 - 文档[15]（同上来源）的模块功能定义表中，F01模块“MCU”的功能定义为“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”。

Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. 问题：板上最主要的电源管理芯片是谁？说清楚输入、三路输出和软件配置出处。
参考答案：U1700 LP87702-Q1 从 VCC3V3 取电，关键输出为 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50，软件配置出处为 HSI 7.2 节。
实际回答：板上最主要的电源管理芯片是 **LP87702-Q1**（具体型号 LP87702RRHBRQ1）。 - **输入电压**：VCC3V3（3.3 V） - 来源：证据 10 和 14 均明确说明“LP87702，VCC3V3 电压输入”。VCC3V3 由系统一级 DC‑DC 从 12 V 车载电池转换而来（证据 10），其标称值为 3.3 V。 - **三路输出**：VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50 - 来源：证据 10 描述“VCC3V3 转 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50”，证据 14 的模块列表同样列出这三路输出。 - **软件配置出处**：文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中的“LP87702 配置”部分，具体为 **Table 5‑2 LP87702 Configuration**。 - 来源：证据 8 指出“LP87702 配置”和“Table 5‑2 LP87702 Configuration”，并说明运行期间需要 MCU 通过 I²C 配置寄存器。 以上信息均基于直接证据，无缺失项。
评分正文：板上最主要的电源管理芯片是 **LP87702-Q1**（具体型号 LP87702RRHBRQ1）。 - **输入电压**：VCC3V3（3.3 V） - 来源：证据 10 和 14 均明确说明“LP87702，VCC3V3 电压输入”。VCC3V3 由系统一级 DC‑DC 从 12 V 车载电池转换而来（证据 10），其标称值为 3.3 V。 - **三路输出**：VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50 - 来源：证据 10 描述“VCC3V3 转 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50”，证据 14 的模块列表同样列出这三路输出。 - **软件配置出处**：文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中的“LP87702 配置”部分，具体为 **Table 5‑2 LP87702 Configuration**。 - 来源：证据 8 指出“LP87702 配置”和“Table 5‑2 LP87702 Configuration”，并说明运行期间需要 MCU 通过 I²C 配置寄存器。 以上信息均基于直接证据，无缺失项。

The VRANGE[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register control which range is active (see Table 9-3). The default output voltage range after device initialization is 0.4 V to 1.675 V in 5-mV steps. Note that every change to the VRANGE[1:0] bits must be followed by a write to the VSET register, even if the value of the VSET[7:o] bits does not change. This sequence is required for the device to start to use the new voltage range. Also note that the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range uses a 0.8-V reference and the other ranges use a 0.4-V reference. Switching to and from the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range can therefore cause increased output voltage undershoot or overshoot while the device switches its internal reference. In device variants that do not have I?C, the output voltage range is factory-set to 0.4 V to 1.675 V. 9.3.6.2OutputVoltageSetpoint Together with the selected range, the VSET[7:0] bits in the VSET register control the output voltage setpoint of the device (see Table 9-4). During initialization, the device reads the state of the VSEL pin and selects the default output voltage according to Table 9-5. Note that the VSEL pin also selects the I?C target address of the device (see Table 9-10). If the user programs new output voltage setpoint (VOUT[7:0]), output voltage range (VRANGE[1:0]), or soft-start time (SSTIME[1:o]) settings when the device has already begun its soft-start sequence, the device ignores the new values until the soft-start sequence is complete. If the user changes the value of VSET[7:0] during soft start, the device first ramps to the value that VSET[7:0] had when the soft-start sequence began. Then, when soft start is complete, ramps up or down to the new value. If the user changes VOUT[7:0], VRAMP[1:0], or SSTIME[1:0] while EN is low, the device uses the new values the next time the user enables it. | During start-up, the output voltage ramps up to the target value set by the VSEL pin before ramping up or down to any new value programmed to the device over the I2C interface. 9.3.6.3Non-DefaultOutputVoltageSetpoint If none of the default voltage range or voltage setpoint combinations are suitable for the application, the user can change these device settings through I2C before the user enables the device. Then, when the user pulls the EN pin high, the device starts up with the desired start-up voltage. Note that if the user changes the device settings through I?C while the device is ramping, the device ignores the changes until the ramp is complete 9.3.6.4 Dynamic Voltage Scaling If the user changes the output voltage setpoint while the DC/DC converter is operating, the device ramps up or down to the new voltage setting in a controlled way. The VRAMP[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register sets the slew rate when the device ramps from one voltage to another during DVS (see Table 9-6). Note that ramping the output to a higher voltage requires additional output current, so that during DvS, the converter must generate a total output current given by: dVour lour = lour c) + Cour dVOouT where · lout is the total current the converter must generate while ramping to a higher voltage. louT(Dc) is the DC load current.· Cout is the total output capacitance. dVout/dt is the slew rate of the output voltage (programmable in the range 0.5 mV/μs to 10 mV/us). For correct operation, ensure that the total output current during DVS does not exceed the current limit of the device. 9.3.7 Compensation (CoMP) The CoMP pin is the connection point for an external compensation network. A series-connected resistor and capacitor to GOSNS is sufficient for typical applications. The series-connected resistor also provides enough scope to optimize the loop response for a wide range of operating conditions. When using multiple devices in a stacked configuration, all devices share a common compensation network, and the COMP pin makes sure there is equal current sharing between them (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.8 ModeSelection and Clock Synchronization (MODE/SYNC) A high level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects forced PWM operation. A low level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects power save operation, in which, the device automatically transitions between PWM and PFM, according to the load conditions. | <table><caption> Table 10-1 lists the operating parameters for this application example. In this case, only a portion of the internal voltage ramp is seen externally (see Figure 9-9). Note that the device always operates in DcM during the start-up ramp, regardless of other configuration settings or operating conditions. 9.3.5SwitchingFrequencySelection During device initialization, a resistor-to-digital converter in the device determines the state of the FSEL pin and sets the switching frequency of the DC/DC converter according to Table 9-2. Detection of the state of the FSEL pin works as follows: To detect the most significant bit (MSB), the circuit opens S1 and S2, and the input buffer detects if a high or a low level is connected to the FSEL pin. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 To detect the least significant bit (LSB): If the MSB was 0, the circuit closes S1. If the input buffer detects a high level, LSB = 1. If the circuit detects a low level, LSB = 0. If the MSB was 1, the circuit closes S2. If the input buffer detects a low level, LSB = 0. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | None：U20; None：P23.2; None：P23.2/GTM_TIM1_IN6_5/GTM_TIM0_IN6_5/GTM_TOUT43/CAN12_TXD; None：GPIO; None：MCU_RST_FLASH_P23.2; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>模块序号 Module number</td><td>模块名称 Module name</td><td>功能定义 Function Tl's customers are responsible for determining suitability of components for their purposes, as well as validating and testing their design implementation to confirm systemfunctionality. 10.1 Application Information The following section discusses selection of the external components to complete the power supply design for typical a application. 10.2 Typical Application 10.2.1 Design Requirements Preliminary Calculations The maximum allowable deviation of the power supply is ±3.3%. The DC accuracy of the TPS6287x-Q1 is specified as ±1%, therefore, the maximum output voltage variation during a transient is given by: VoUT = ± VouT × (3.3% - 1%) = ±17.25 mV 10.2.2 Detailed Design Procedure The following subsections describe how to calculate the external components required to meet the specified transient requirements of a given application. The calculations include the worst-case variation of components and use the RMS method to combine the variation of uncorrelated parameters. 10.2.2.1 Selecting the Inductor The TPS6287x-Q1 devices have been optimized for inductors in the range 50 nH to 300 nH. If the transient response of the converter is limited by the slew rate of the current in the inductor, using a smaller inductor can improve performance. However, the output ripple current increases as the value of the inductor decreases and higher output current ripple generates higher output voltage ripple, which adds to the transient overshoot or undershoot. The optimal configuration for a given application is always a trade-off between a number of parameters. Tl recommends a starting value of 110 nH for typical applications. | Updated theDevice OptionsTable. 3 .5 Modified MODE/SYNC pin Description in Pin Functions Changed the title of Figure 8-5 12 Added approximate minimum on time value. 15 Added behavior of device when EN is toggled and input voltage is applied.. .17 · Modified Table 9-5 to reflect new available Orderable Part Number.. 21 Added a comment on how to connect secondary devices SYNC_OUT pin.. 27 Added a footnote to Table 9-10 .31 Added I2C Register Reset .34 Added Number of phases NΦ and adapted passive component selection equations in Typical Application ...41 Page Changes from Revision * (May 2022) to Revision A (July 2022) Changed document status from Advance Information to Production Data...... .1 5 Description (continued) The switching frequency is resistor-selectable through the FSEL pin. The switching frequency can be set to 1.5 MHz, 2.25 MHz, 2.5 MHz, or 3.0 MHz, or synchronized to an external clock in the same frequency range. The I2C-compatible interface offers several control, monitoring, and warning features, such as voltage monitoring and temperature-related warnings. The output voltage can be quickly adjusted to adapt the power consumption of the load to the performance needs of the application through the I2C-compatible interface. The default start-up voltage is resistor-selectable through the VSEL pin. 6 Device Options The I2C address is linked to the selected start-up voltage.
