The output current rating of the input power supply must be rated according to the supply voltage and current requirements of the TPS6287X-Q1 10.5Layout 10.5.1 Layout Guidelines Achieving the performance the TPS6287x-Q1 devices are capable of requires proper PDN and PCB design. TI therefore recommends the user perform a power integrity analysis on their design. There are a number of commercially available power integrity software tools, and the user can use these tools to model the effects on performance of the PCB layout and passive components. In addition to the use of power integrity tools, Tl recommends the following basic principles: · Place the input capacitors close to the VIN and GND pins. Position the input capacitors in order of increasing size, starting with the smallest capacitors closest to the VIN and GND pins. Use an identical layout for both VIN-GND pin pairs of the package, to gain maximum benefit from the butterfly configuration. ·Place the inductor close to the device and keep the SW node small. · Connect the exposed thermal pad and the GND pins of the device together. Use multiple thermal vias to connect the exposed thermal pad of the device to one or more ground planes (Tl's EVM uses nine 150-μm thermal vias). ·Use multiple power and ground planes. · Route the VOSNS and GOSNS remote sense lines as a differential pair and connect them to the lowest- impedance point of the PDN. If the desired connection point is not the lowest impedance point of the PDN, optimize the PDN until it is. Do not route the VOSNS and GOSNS close to any of the switch nodes. · Connect the compensation components between VOSNS and GOSNS. Do not connect the compensation components directly to power ground. · If possible, distribute the output capacitors evenly between the TPS6287x-Q1 device and the point-of-load, rather than placing them altogether in one place. · Use multiple vias to connect each capacitor pad to the power and ground planes (TI's EVM typically uses four vias per pad). Use plenty of stitching vias to ensure a low impedance connection between different power and ground· planes. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 | 9.4.4 0n The device operates in on mode when the supply voltage is greater than the UVLO threshold and all of the following conditions are true: ·A high-level is applied to the EN pin. SWEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The device junction temperature is below the thermal shutdown threshold.· · The supply voltage is below the OVLO threshold. All functions are available in on mode. The device leaves on mode and enters UVLO mode when the supply voltage decreases below the UVLO threshold. The device leaves on mode and enters standby mode when any of the following conditions is true: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. · The device junction temperature is greater than the thermal shutdown threshold. ·The supply voltage is greater than the OVLO threshold. 9.5 Programming 9.5.1 Serial Interface Description I2C is a 2-wire serial interface developed by Philips Semiconductor, now NXP Semiconductors (see I2C-Bus Specification and User Manual, Revision 6, 4 April 2014). The bus consists of a data line (SDA) and a clock line (SCL) with pullup structures. When the bus is idle, both SDA and SCL lines are pulled high. All I2C-compatible devices connect to the I2C bus through open-drain I/O pins, SDA and SCL. A controller, usually a microcontroller or a digital signal processor, controls the bus. The controller is responsible for generating the SCL signal and device addresses. The controller also generates specific conditions that indicate the START and STOP of data transfer. A target receives data, transmits data, or both on the bus under control of the controller. The TPS6287x-Q1 device operates as a target and supports the following data transfer modes, as defined in the I2C-Bus Specification: standard mode (100 kbps), fast mode (400 kbps), and fast mode plus (1 Mbps). The interface adds flexibility to the power supply solution, enabling most functions to be programmed to new values depending on the instantaneous application requirements. Register contents remain intact as long as the input voltage remains above 1.4 V. The data transfer protocol for standard and fast modes is exactly the same, therefore, they are referred to as F/S-mode in this document. The device supports 7-bit addressing; general call addresses are not supported. | IMPORTANTNOTICEANDDISCLAIMER TI PROVIDES TECHNICAL AND RELIABILITY DATA (INCLUDING DATA SHEETS), DESIGN RESOURCES (INCLUDING REFERENCE DESIGNS), APPLICATION OR OTHER DESIGN ADVICE, WEB TOOLS, SAFETY INFORMATION, AND OTHER RESOURCES “AS IS" AND WITH ALL FAULTS, AND DISCLAIMS ALL WARRANTIES, EXPRESS AND IMPLIED, INCLUDING WITHOUT LIMITATION ANY IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY, FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE OR NON-INFRINGEMENT OF THIRD PARTY INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY RIGHTS. 问题：U1800 的输入、输出检测、使能和 I²C 控制网络分别是什么？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 为 VCC3V3，VOSNS 为 VCC0V75，EN 接 N19888813，并通过连接到 MCU 的 SDA/SCL 进行 I²C 控制。
实际回答：根据U1800的引脚映射，结论如下： - **输入网络**：VCC3V3（连接到VIN引脚）。 - **输出网络**：N19808656（连接到SW引脚）。 - **使能网络**：N19888813（连接到EN引脚）。 - **I²C控制网络**：SDA为MCU_SDA_P15.5，SCL为MCU_SCL_P15.4（分别连接到SDA和SCL引脚）。 **证据**：来源为电路设计文件“825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF”中的U1800引脚映射，明确列出了各引脚对应的网络名称。 **缺失信息**：证据中未提及“输出检测”相关的网络或功能，无法确认U1800是否存在输出检测网络或该功能的具体连接。
评分正文：根据U1800的引脚映射，结论如下： - **输入网络**：VCC3V3（连接到VIN引脚）。 - **输出网络**：N19808656（连接到SW引脚）。 - **使能网络**：N19888813（连接到EN引脚）。 - **I²C控制网络**：SDA为MCU_SDA_P15.5，SCL为MCU_SCL_P15.4（分别连接到SDA和SCL引脚）。 **证据**：来源为电路设计文件“825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF”中的U1800引脚映射，明确列出了各引脚对应的网络名称。 **缺失信息**：证据中未提及“输出检测”相关的网络或功能，无法确认U1800是否存在输出检测网络或该功能的具体连接。

问题：VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过哪颗负载开关，使能信号是什么？
参考答案：VCC1V8 经 U1803（TPS22918）输出为 VCC1V8_EQ，使能信号为 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过的负载开关位号为 **U1803**，其使能信号为 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**。当前证据未提供该负载开关的具体型号。 - **负载开关**：位号 U1803，其输出引脚 VOUT 和 QOD 连接至网络 VCC1V8_EQ（证据13）。 - **使能信号**：网络 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN 连接至 U1803 的 ON 引脚（证据12）。 **缺失信息**：U1803 的器件型号未在已有证据中明确给出。
评分正文：VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过的负载开关位号为 **U1803**，其使能信号为 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**。当前证据未提供该负载开关的具体型号。 - **负载开关**：位号 U1803，其输出引脚 VOUT 和 QOD 连接至网络 VCC1V8_EQ（证据13）。 - **使能信号**：网络 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN 连接至 U1803 的 ON 引脚（证据12）。 **缺失信息**：U1803 的器件型号未在已有证据中明确给出。

Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. 问题：文档声明板上有哪些 CAN 相关模块，回答时能否用网表确认其具体连接？
参考答案：HSI 硬件架构包含 CAN/CAN FD 电路；具体器件和网络连接应以 EDF 检索结果为准，文档能力声明与网表拓扑需分别标注来源。
实际回答：**直接结论：** 文档声明板上有四个CAN相关模块，分别是CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3。网表可以确认其部分具体连接，包括CAN差分信号线、唤醒信号以及控制信号。 **证据：** 1. **文档中列出的CAN相关模块：** * 根据文档[13]中的模块列表，声明了以下四个CAN模块： * F23: CAN0 * F22: CAN1 * F21: CAN2 * F20: CAN3 * 文档[14]进一步说明，这四个模块均用于CAN/CAN FD通讯，支持最大速率5Mbps，并兼容TCAN1145和TCAN1044A两种收发器方案。 2. **网表中CAN模块的具体连接：** * 网表[1]至[12]显示了CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3的差分信号线（如CAN0H、CAN0L）均连接到连接器X1900的对应引脚。 * 网表[3]和[8]显示了CAN0和CAN1的唤醒信号（CAN0_INH、CAN1_INH）连接到收发器的INH引脚。 * 网表[5]显示了CAN0的控制信号（CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8）连接到收发器U1200的SCSN引脚、U1202的STB引脚和U1203的NSTB引脚。
评分正文：**直接结论：** 文档声明板上有四个CAN相关模块，分别是CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3。网表可以确认其部分具体连接，包括CAN差分信号线、唤醒信号以及控制信号。 **证据：** 1. **文档中列出的CAN相关模块：** * 根据文档[13]中的模块列表，声明了以下四个CAN模块： * F23: CAN0 * F22: CAN1 * F21: CAN2 * F20: CAN3 * 文档[14]进一步说明，这四个模块均用于CAN/CAN FD通讯，支持最大速率5Mbps，并兼容TCAN1145和TCAN1044A两种收发器方案。 2. **网表中CAN模块的具体连接：** * 网表[1]至[12]显示了CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3的差分信号线（如CAN0H、CAN0L）均连接到连接器X1900的对应引脚。 * 网表[3]和[8]显示了CAN0和CAN1的唤醒信号（CAN0_INH、CAN1_INH）连接到收发器的INH引脚。 * 网表[5]显示了CAN0的控制信号（CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8）连接到收发器U1200的SCSN引脚、U1202的STB引脚和U1203的NSTB引脚。

When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。 VCCIV8 is used to power EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH.VCC1V1 is used to power EQ6,DDR. VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, now choose LP87702 PMIC scheme. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 采用LP87702芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50，其中VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1输出电压和使能。用MCU的P12.1引脚复位该芯片。 LP87702 chip is used to convert VCC3V3 into VCC1V8, VCC1V1 and VCC50, in which VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR and FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6 and DDR power supply, and VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supplyP15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1 output voltage and enable it. Reset the chip with P12.1 pin of the MCU. </td></tr></table> | 顾客代表Customer Representative其他与项目相关人员等Other relevant person术语及缩写解释 Terms and AbbreviationsTable 1-1Terms and Abbreviations参考文档 Reference DocumentTable 1-2Reference Document硬件架构 Hardware Architecture硬件架构描述了60060894_ADAS项目的硬件基础模块，模块和模块之间的接口。The Hardware Architecture describe the HW basic component of 600608964_ADAS project, as well as the interface of each component.系统概述 System overviewADAS产品要求功能安全达到ASIL-B，硬件整体架构介绍如下：ADAS products require functional safety to meet ASIL-B. The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system block功能安全分解相关模块模块电路 Module circuitKL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F02/F05/F10</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>对EQ6和LPDDR4周边PCB温度进行监控。AOU需求MCU监控EQ6芯片内结温，确保EQ6工作安全。AOU需求EQ6监控CAM的工作温度，要求温度监控功能达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统的功能安全。 The PCB temperature around EQ6 and LPDDR4 was monitored. AOU requires MCU to monitor the junction temperature inside EQ6 chip to ensure the safety of EQ6. AOU requires EQ6 to monitor the operating temperature of the CAM, and the temperature monitoring function is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the functional safety of the EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td> </td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6温度采样 EQ6-L内部内置了两个温度传感器，并通过外部SPI接口实时发送给MCU，硬件设计可以支持MCU对EQ6结温的监控。 1, EQ6 temperature sampling EQ6-l has two internal temperature sensors, which are sent to the MCU in real time through the external SPI interface. The hardware design can support the MCU to monitor the junction temperature of EQ6. NTC温度采样 硬件设计使用温敏电阻NTC（NCU15XH103F6SRC）采集EQ6/LPDDR4周边环境温度，并通过MCU进行ADC采样和实时监控。NTC温度监控范围：-40~150℃，监控精度±3℃@-40~150℃，MCU的ADC为10bit，即1024个量阶，使得监测精度可以达到3.2mV，电压采集范围0-3.3V。用MCU的AN7引脚采样。 2.NTC temperature sampling In hardware design, the temperature sensor NTC (NCU15XH103F6SRC) is used to collect the ambient temperature of EQ6/LPDDR4, and the ADC sampling and real-time monitoring are carried out by MCU. NTC temperature monitoring range: -40-150℃, monitoring accuracy ±3℃@-40-150℃, MCU ADC is 10bit, that is, 1024 quantity order, so that the monitoring accuracy can reach 3.2mV, voltage acquisition range 0-3.3V. The AN7 pin of the MCU is used for sampling. CAM温度采样 CAM的IMX-728内置了两个温度传感器，温度测量范围是：-40~125℃，温度监控精度为±5℃@-40~125℃。EYEQ6可以通过I2C接口（EQ6L的I2C_0_SCLK和I2C_0_SDA引脚）实时读取IMX-728的工作温度，并进行状态监控和异常诊断。 3.CAM temperature sampling CAM IMX-728 built-in two temperature sensors, temperature measurement range is: -40~125℃, temperature monitoring accuracy of ±5℃@-40~125℃. EYEQ6 can read the operating temperature of the IMX-728 in real time through the I2C interface (I2C_0_SCLK and I2C_0_SDA pins of EQ6L), and perform condition monitoring and anomaly diagnosis. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table>

问题：仅根据网表，3V3 过压保护的精确触发阈值是多少？
参考答案：网表只能表明连接拓扑，不能给出精确保护阈值；当前缺少对应数据手册或规格证据，应明确无法确认。
实际回答：根据网表，3V3过压保护的精确触发阈值为 **4.03V**。 **支撑证据**： - 文档 [9]（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）中模块 F18 的“模块方案”明确说明： *“当 VCC3V3 > 4.03V 时，cathode 引脚输出逻辑低（最高1V），NMOS 关闭，PMOS 关闭，VCC3V3_SW 无电，对后级负载起到过压保护作用。”* 该文档直接定义过压保护触发条件为 VCC3V3 超过 4.03V。 **说明**： - 网表（如网络 VCC3V3、VCC3V3_EQ、&3V3_EQ_ACQ 等）仅提供了电路连接关系，未包含分压电阻或比较器参数，因此无法从网表本身计算出阈值。阈值信息来源于设计文档，属于“模块方案”描述内容。 - 未发现其他冲突证据。
评分正文：根据网表，3V3过压保护的精确触发阈值为 **4.03V**。 **支撑证据**： - 文档 [9]（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）中模块 F18 的“模块方案”明确说明： *“当 VCC3V3 > 4.03V 时，cathode 引脚输出逻辑低（最高1V），NMOS 关闭，PMOS 关闭，VCC3V3_SW 无电，对后级负载起到过压保护作用。”* 该文档直接定义过压保护触发条件为 VCC3V3 超过 4.03V。 **说明**： - 网表（如网络 VCC3V3、VCC3V3_EQ、&3V3_EQ_ACQ 等）仅提供了电路连接关系，未包含分压电阻或比较器参数，因此无法从网表本身计算出阈值。阈值信息来源于设计文档，属于“模块方案”描述内容。 - 未发现其他冲突证据。

Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | 注：使用本模板时请先阅读填写说明页，形成工作产品时本页不可删除变更历史Change History目 录Table of Contents1 概述 Introduction 81.1 目的 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | None：MCU采样以太网的VCC3V3_ETH电源电压 精度要求：3.135V～3.465V; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：QM; None：W8; None：AN8; None：AN8/EVADC_G11CH4/EVADC_G1CH0; None：ADC，分辨率10bit; None：3V3_ETH_ACQ; None：/; None：I; None：ETH 3V3电压采样 U3V3_ETH_ACQ=3.3*ADC值/1024 U3V3_ETH=U3V3_ETH_ACQ/分压比，分压比=1/2 精度要求：3.3V±10%; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 HW Interface Component ：B; None：F08/EQ system power sample1; None：MCU采样EQ6L系统的VCC3V3_EQ电源电压 精度要求：3V3±5%; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：B; None：W9; None：AN2; None：AN2/EVADC_G0CH2/EDSADC_EDS0PA; None：ADC，分辨率10bit; None：3V3_EQ_ACQ; None：/; None：I; None：EQ 3V3电压采样 U3V3_EQ_ACQ=3.3*ADC值/1024 U3V3_EQ=U3V3_EQ_ACQ/分压比，分压比=1/2 精度要求：3.3V±5%; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 None：MCU采样EQ6L系统的VCC1V8电源电压 精度要求：1V8±5%; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：B; None：U9; None：AN3; None：AN3/EVADC_G0CH3/EDSADC_EDS0NA; None：ADC，分辨率10bit; None：1V8_ACQ; None：/; None：I; None：1V8电压采样 U1V8_ACQ=3.3*ADC值/1024 精度：1V8±5% 硬件滤波截止频率：≥121.7Hz 硬件滤波时间：≤1.6ms; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F06/F11</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS正常工作电压范围9~16VDC，系统需求需要对产品供电KL30进行电源管理，硬件电路对UBD电源网络进行ADC采样。 ADAS normal operating voltage range is 9~16VDC. The hardware circuit performs ADC sampling on the UBD power supply.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>MCU_UB_SW_P00.10信号是由MCU I/O控制的数字信号，用于使能KL30电压采样功能。 MCU_UB_SW_P00.10 signal is a digital signal controlled by MCU I/O, used to enable KL30 voltage sampling function. UBSW_ACQ_AN6信号是UBD经过100K 1%、10K 1%电阻分压后的模拟信号。 UBSW_ACQ_AN6 signal is an analog signal of UBD divided by 100K 1%、10K 1% resistors. Which is connected to the ADC pin of MCU for sampling. MCU的ADC采集到UBD后进行电源管理，当软件诊断到UBD电压范围异常时，MCU会关断EQ和其他负载工作电源，确保ADAS功能安全。 The ADC of MCU collects UBD for power management. When the software detects that UBD voltage range is abnormal, the MCU will turn off EQ and other load power supplies to ensure the functional safety of ADAS.</td></tr></table>

READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. Description 1 Features AEC-Q100 qualified for automotive applications The TPS6287x-Q1 is a family of pin-to-pin 6-A, 9- Temperature grade 1: -40°C to +125°C, TA A, 12-A, and 15-A synchronous step-down DC/DC converters with differential remote sensing. For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation | <table> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >MIN</td><td >NOM</td><td >MAX</td><td >UNIT</td></tr> <tr><td >VIN</td><td >Input voltage</td><td >VIN SDA, SCL</td><td >2.7</td><td></td><td >6 5</td><td >V</td></tr> <tr><td >VouT</td><td >Output voltage</td><td></td><td >0.4</td><td></td><td >3.35 V or (ViN - 1.4</td><td></td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >V)(1)</td><td></td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td >TPS62870-Q1</td><td></td><td></td><td >6</td><td></td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td >TPS62871-Q1</td><td></td><td></td><td >9</td><td></td></tr> <tr><td >loUT</td><td >Output current</td><td >TPS62872-Q1</td><td></td><td></td><td >12</td><td >A</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td >TPS62873-Q1</td><td></td><td></td><td >15</td><td></td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >Inductance</td><td></td><td >110</td><td></td><td >330</td><td >nH</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td >fsw≥2.25MHz andVouT≤1.675V</td><td >55</td><td></td><td >330</td><td></td></tr> </table> | 9 Detailed Description 9.1overview The TPS6287x-Q1 devices are synchronous step-down (buck) DC/DC converters. These devices use an enhanced DCS control topology to achieve fast transient response while switching with a fixed frequency. Together, with their low output voltage ripple, high DC accuracy, and differential remote sensing, these devices are ideal for supplying the cores of modern high-performance processors. The family of devices includes 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, and 15-A devices. To further increase the output current capability, the user can combine multiple devices in a “stack". For example, a stack of two TPS62873-Q1 devices have a current capability of 30 A. Each device of a stack must have the same current rating to avoid that one device enters current limit too early. For each current rating, there are full-featured devices with an I2C interface and limited-featured devices without an I2C interface (see the Device Options). The user can use a device variant without I2C in exactly the same way as a device variant with I?C, except that: · The user must connect the unused SCL and SDA pins to GND · The user must be aware of the (fixed) factory settings for parameters and functions that are programmable in the I²C device variants. 9.2 Functional Block Diagram 9.3FeatureDescription 9.3.1 Fixed-Frequency DCS Control Topology TPS6287x-Q1 devices. This topology is comprised of an inner emulated current loop, a middle direct feedback loop, and an outer voltage-regulating loop. 9.3.2 Forced PwM and Power Save Modes The device can control the inductor current in three different ways to regulate the output: Pulse-width modulation with continuous inductor current (PWM-CCM) Pulse-width modulation with discontinuous inductor current (PWM-DCM) Pulse-frequency modulation with discontinuous inductor current and pulse skipping (PFM-DCM) During PWM-CCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is continuous (see Figure 9-2). PwM operation achieves the lowest output voltage ripple and the best transient performance. During PWM-DCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is discontinuous (see Figure 9-3). In this mode, the device controls the peak inductor current to maintain the selected switching frequency while still being able to regulate the output.

问题：VCC3V3 在板上主要给哪些电源器件供电？不要把电阻电容当成主要负载。
参考答案：VCC3V3 是主要 3.3V 电源轨，供给 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600 等 IC；回答应聚焦芯片而非无源器件。
实际回答：**直接结论：** VCC3V3 在板上主要给以下电源器件供电：LP87702（PMIC）、TPS62872（DC-DC）、SA23002（LDO/电源转换芯片）、以及两个 TPS22918 负载开关（分别产生 VCC3V3_EQ 和 VCC3V3_ETH）。 **支撑证据：** - 文档 [8] 中的表格明确列出： - LP87702：VCC3V3 电压输入，输出 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50。 - TPS62872：VCC3V3 电压输入，输出 VCC0V75。 - SA23002：VCC3V3 输入，输出 0V75_MIPI_DDR。 - TPS22918 负载开关（F16）：VCC3V3 电压输入，输出 VCC3V3_EQ。 - TPS22918 负载开关（F17）：VCC3V3 输入，输出 VCC3V3_ETH。 - 文档 [9]、[10] 分别确认了 F17（TPS22918，VCC3V3 转 VCC3V3_ETH）和 F16（TPS22918，VCC3V3 转 VCC3V3_EQ）的负载开关方案。 - 文档 [12] 中的模块列表进一步验证了上述五个电源模块（F13、F14、F15、F16、F17）的输入均为 VCC3V3。 **缺口说明：** - 缺少电路图（如 825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）中具体器件位号（如 U1800、U1804 等）与上述芯片型号的直接对应关系，无法确认电路图中哪些具体实例对应 LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002。 - 此外，VCC3V3 网络还连接了 U1600、U1700 等器件，但现有证据未明确其是否为电源器件，因此未纳入结论。

ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F17</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH is used for ETH power supply, and the load switching scheme is now selected.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC3V3系统电源转换为VCC3V3_ETH，提供给MCU的ETH供电。P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 The TPS22918 load switch is used to convert the VCC3V3 system power to VCC3V3-ETH to supply power to ETH of the MCU. 问题：软件配置 LP87702 应查 HSI 的哪一节？
参考答案：应查 HSI 7.2 节“LP87702 配置”，其中给出软件寄存器和 I²C 配置说明。
实际回答：**直接结论：** 软件配置 LP87702 应查 HSI 文档（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）的 **第7.2节 “LP87702配置”**。 **支撑证据：** 1. HSI文档的目录结构明确指出，第7章“其他芯片配置”包含第7.2节“LP87702配置”（来源：[3]）。 2. 该节（7.2节）内容明确说明：“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”（来源：[2]）。
评分正文：**直接结论：** 软件配置 LP87702 应查 HSI 文档（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）的 **第7.2节 “LP87702配置”**。 **支撑证据：** 1. HSI文档的目录结构明确指出，第7章“其他芯片配置”包含第7.2节“LP87702配置”（来源：[3]）。 2. 该节（7.2节）内容明确说明：“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”（来源：[2]）。

READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | None：U20; 问题：网表写 TC367、接口文档写 TC377，这两个说法怎么处理？
参考答案：必须保留来源差异：EDF 的 U900 型号是 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，而 HSI 章节名是 TC377 最小系统；现有证据不足以把二者无条件视为同一准确型号。
实际回答：**结论：网表中存在以“TC367”命名的网络节点，接口文档中描述的是TC377（具体型号为SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA），但两者关系未经证实，且存在证据缺口。** 以下是支撑该结论的详细分析，按子问题组织： ### 1. 网表中“TC367”出现的位置 - **结论**：网表中“TC367”出现在多个JTAG和复位信号的网络名称中，如 `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`、`TC367_JTAG_TDI`、`TC367_JTAG_TDO/DAP2`、`TC367_JTAG_TMS/DAP1`、`TC367_JTAG_TRST` 和 `TC367_RESET_N`。 - **证据来源**：电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`（证据[1]至[6]）中明确记录了这些网络，并连接到 `X2000`、`U900B` 等器件。 ### 2. 接口文档中“TC377”出现的位置 - **结论**：接口文档中明确使用了“TC377”及其完整型号“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”来描述主控芯片（MCU）。 - **证据来源**：在 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 文档中： - **表3.2 系统功能矩阵**（证据[8]）中，模块F01的描述为“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”。 - **模块功能描述**（证据[15]）中，明确说明“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片...”。 - **其他部分**（证据[10]、[14]）多次提及“TC377最小系统”。 ### 3. “TC367”与“TC377”的关系 - **结论**：现有证据中，**没有直接证据**表明网表中的“TC367”与接口文档中的“TC377”是兼容替换关系、版本迭代或明确别名。两者在各自文档中独立出现，但均指向同一个电路设计中的主控芯片模块（如网表中的 `X2000` 和接口文档中的 `F01/MCU`）。 - **证据来源**：所有证据均未同时提及这两个型号，也未提供任何关于两者关系的说明。例如，连接至 `TC367_RESET_N` 网络的 `U900B` 器件（证据[6]），与接口文档中描述的MCU模块（证据[15]）在功能上可能对应，但缺乏型号关联的直接证明。 ### 缺失信息 - **关键缺口**：接口文档中**未直接出现“TC377”这一简写字符串**，而是以完整型号“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”出现。此外，**没有任何文档**解释网表为何使用“TC367”命名网络，而接口文档使用“TC377”描述器件。因此，两者之间的关系完全未知，可能是设计阶段的命名差异，也可能是不同型号的器件，但无法确认。
评分正文：**结论：网表中存在以“TC367”命名的网络节点，接口文档中描述的是TC377（具体型号为SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA），但两者关系未经证实，且存在证据缺口。** 以下是支撑该结论的详细分析，按子问题组织： ### 1. 网表中“TC367”出现的位置 - **结论**：网表中“TC367”出现在多个JTAG和复位信号的网络名称中，如 `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`、`TC367_JTAG_TDI`、`TC367_JTAG_TDO/DAP2`、`TC367_JTAG_TMS/DAP1`、`TC367_JTAG_TRST` 和 `TC367_RESET_N`。 - **证据来源**：电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`（证据[1]至[6]）中明确记录了这些网络，并连接到 `X2000`、`U900B` 等器件。 ### 2. 接口文档中“TC377”出现的位置 - **结论**：接口文档中明确使用了“TC377”及其完整型号“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”来描述主控芯片（MCU）。 - **证据来源**：在 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 文档中： - **表3.2 系统功能矩阵**（证据[8]）中，模块F01的描述为“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”。 - **模块功能描述**（证据[15]）中，明确说明“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片...”。 - **其他部分**（证据[10]、[14]）多次提及“TC377最小系统”。 ### 3. “TC367”与“TC377”的关系 - **结论**：现有证据中，**没有直接证据**表明网表中的“TC367”与接口文档中的“TC377”是兼容替换关系、版本迭代或明确别名。两者在各自文档中独立出现，但均指向同一个电路设计中的主控芯片模块（如网表中的 `X2000` 和接口文档中的 `F01/MCU`）。 - **证据来源**：所有证据均未同时提及这两个型号，也未提供任何关于两者关系的说明。例如，连接至 `TC367_RESET_N` 网络的 `U900B` 器件（证据[6]），与接口文档中描述的MCU模块（证据[15]）在功能上可能对应，但缺乏型号关联的直接证明。 ### 缺失信息 - **关键缺口**：接口文档中**未直接出现“TC377”这一简写字符串**，而是以完整型号“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”出现。此外，**没有任何文档**解释网表为何使用“TC367”命名网络，而接口文档使用“TC377”描述器件。因此，两者之间的关系完全未知，可能是设计阶段的命名差异，也可能是不同型号的器件，但无法确认。

solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | Sheet名称：9_SA; 内容摘要：xxx系列产品系统层级假设; 项目适配说明：需要需求开发人员确认本sheet的 D列是否被满足，并填写确认结果 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：10_Recognition Unit; 内容摘要：xxx的硬件组件图; 项目适配说明：需要根据实际的项目需要， 确认硬件架构图 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：11_xxxxxB(D)_HWFMEA; 内容摘要：xxx的硬件FMEA; 项目适配说明：须要需求开发人员结合项目的硬件原理图和安全需求，完善FMEA内容，以判断xxx中的硬件组件在本项目中是否安全相关，并与TSR建立追溯关系 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：12_xxxxxB(D)_SIR; 内容摘要：xxx的安全机制及分配给 软硬件的需求; 项目适配说明：基于FMEA结果，在确认需求后，需要结合客户释放的安全需求和"Registers Bit Description_13"sheet页check需求，并明确本sheet中红色字体部分的内容 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：13_Registers Bit Description; 内容摘要：需求确认中所需的相关寄存器信息汇总列表; 项目适配说明：需要需求开发人员在确认需求时 了解相关寄存器 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：14_xxxxxB(D)_DTCAndDID Table; 内容摘要：不同故障的DTC和DID; 项目适配说明：对于每一种故障，需要明确DTC和DID为多少 ——0_Instruction Manual Unnamed: 0：nan; Unnamed: 1：nan; Unnamed: 2：nan; 文件编号：：ID: ; APXXXXXX_XXX_FS_SM_XXX：APXXXXXX_XXX_FS_SM_XXX ——1_Face | ADC电压采集阈值 The ADC voltage acquisition thresholdWCCA计算结果如下，软件应按照如下过欠压阈值设置：The WCCA calculation results are as follows. 评分上下文：8/30 段，1691/25484 字符；已裁剪：是
hw-v1-joint-pmic-noisy-querysuccess0.2780.5820.714not_applicable0.1250.5000.5000.500not_applicable
问题：忽略无关的摄像头和 CAN 内容，只结合电路与 HSI 说明 LP87702 的供电输出和配置。
参考答案：LP87702-Q1 是 U1700，输入 VCC3V3，关键输出为 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50；配置依据 HSI 7.2 节，回答不应混入无关摄像头或 CAN 结论。
实际回答：**结论** LP87702 的供电输出和配置信息如下： 1. **供电输出（部分证据，存在缺口）**：LP87702 将 VCC3V3 输入转换为三路输出：**VCC1V8**、**VCC1V1**、**VCC50**。证据来源：HSI 文档（[8]、[11]、[12]）。 * **缺口**：缺少 LP87702 供电输出引脚（如 BUCK1、BUCK2、LDO）及其在电路原理图中的具体网络连接信息。证据[1] 仅确认了电路设计中存在该器件实例（U1700），未提供引脚级连接细节。 2. **配置信息**：LP87702 的运行模式和寄存器配置如下： * **运行模式**：支持 SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE 四种模式。其模式切换由 VANA 电压、NRST 引脚电平及特定使能信号（如 MCU_OV75_MIPI_DDR_EN）控制。证据来源：HSI 文档（[2]）。 * **寄存器配置**：MCU 需通过 I2C 通信（地址为 0x08）对 LP87702 进行配置。证据来源：HSI 文档（[9]）。 * 通过 MCU 的 GPIO（P15.3）控制 VCC50 输出的使能/关断。证据来源：HSI 文档（[8]）。 * 通过 I2C 控制 VCC1V8 和 VCC1V1 输出的使能/关断及输出电压设置。证据来源：HSI 文档（[8]、[11]）。 * 预留中断信号（MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1）用于 MCU 监控 LP87702 工作状态。证据来源：HSI 文档（[9]）。 * 看门狗喂狗参数（长周期 600ms，窗口 10-30ms，复位低电平持续 10ms）由 LP87702 的 OTP 文件配置，通过 MCU 的 P13.3 引脚喂狗。证据来源：HSI 文档（[15]）。
评分正文：**结论** LP87702 的供电输出和配置信息如下： 1. **供电输出（部分证据，存在缺口）**：LP87702 将 VCC3V3 输入转换为三路输出：**VCC1V8**、**VCC1V1**、**VCC50**。证据来源：HSI 文档（[8]、[11]、[12]）。 * **缺口**：缺少 LP87702 供电输出引脚（如 BUCK1、BUCK2、LDO）及其在电路原理图中的具体网络连接信息。证据[1] 仅确认了电路设计中存在该器件实例（U1700），未提供引脚级连接细节。 2. **配置信息**：LP87702 的运行模式和寄存器配置如下： * **运行模式**：支持 SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE 四种模式。其模式切换由 VANA 电压、NRST 引脚电平及特定使能信号（如 MCU_OV75_MIPI_DDR_EN）控制。证据来源：HSI 文档（[2]）。 * **寄存器配置**：MCU 需通过 I2C 通信（地址为 0x08）对 LP87702 进行配置。证据来源：HSI 文档（[9]）。 * 通过 MCU 的 GPIO（P15.3）控制 VCC50 输出的使能/关断。证据来源：HSI 文档（[8]）。 * 通过 I2C 控制 VCC1V8 和 VCC1V1 输出的使能/关断及输出电压设置。证据来源：HSI 文档（[8]、[11]）。 * 预留中断信号（MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1）用于 MCU 监控 LP87702 工作状态。证据来源：HSI 文档（[9]）。 * 看门狗喂狗参数（长周期 600ms，窗口 10-30ms，复位低电平持续 10ms）由 LP87702 的 OTP 文件配置，通过 MCU 的 P13.3 引脚喂狗。证据来源：HSI 文档（[15]）。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F26</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求MCU外置看门狗，用于监控MCU功能异常时进行复位。 The system requires MCU external watchdog, used to monitor MCU function abnormal reset.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>窗口看门狗喂狗参数由LP87702的OTP文件进行配置，具体参数为：喂狗长周期600ms，窗口为10~30ms，看门狗输出复位信号低电平持续10ms。用MCU的P13.3引脚喂狗。 The window watchdog feeding parameters are configured by the OTP file of LP87702, the specific parameters are: feeding the dog for a long period of 600ms, the window is 10~30ms, and the low level of the watchdog output reset signal lasts for 10ms. Feed the dog with P13.3 pin of the MCU. </td></tr></table>

问题：从 MCU 使能开始，追踪到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的完整路径。
参考答案：MCU_3V3_ETH_EN 使能 U1500，VCC3V3 经 U1500 形成 VCC3V3_ETH，再进入 U1501 TPS74501，输出 VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：基于现有证据，无法完整追踪从 MCU 使能到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的路径。路径中存在关键缺口：从 MCU 使能信号输出到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源生成之间的中间器件（如电源管理 IC、LDO）及其连接关系缺失。 **子问题 1：MCU 使能引脚是哪个？其连接的信号名称或网络是什么？** **结论：** MCU 的使能引脚为 **P20.0** 和 **P20.3**，其连接的使能信号网络分别为 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN** 和 **MCU_3V3_EQ_EN**。 **支撑证据：** * 证据 [13] 指出，网络 `MCU_1V8_EQ_EN` 连接了 MCU 引脚 `U900E.P20.0` 和器件 `U1803.ON` (使能引脚)。 * 证据 [14] 指出，网络 `MCU_3V3_EQ_EN` 连接了 MCU 引脚 `U900E.P20.3` 和器件 `U1802.ON` (使能引脚)。 **子问题 2：使能信号经过哪些中间器件（如电源管理IC、LDO）？这些器件的使能输入和输出关系如何？** **结论：** **缺口。** 现有证据无法确定 MCU 使能信号 `MCU_1V8_EQ_EN` 或 `MCU_3V3_EQ_EN` 与以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源生成路径之间存在任何中间器件及其连接关系。 **缺失信息：** 未找到任何证据描述使能信号经过的电源管理 IC、LDO 或它们的输入/输出关系。 **子问题 3：以太网PHY的1.0V电源输入引脚是哪个？其电源树上游节点是什么？** **结论：** 以太网 PHY (U1400) 的电源输入引脚为 **VDDIO** 和 **VDDMAC**，它们连接到同一个网络 **N22956687**。**缺口。** 现有证据无法确定网络 `N22956687` 的上游电源节点是什么。 **支撑证据：** * 证据 [1] 显示，U1400 的 `VDDIO` 和 `VDDMAC` 引脚连接到网络 `N22956687`。 **子问题 4：使能信号如何控制或影响以太网PHY 1.0V电源的生成？完整的路径包括哪些节点？** **结论：** **路径不完整，存在关键缺口。** 由于子问题 2 的中间器件环节完全缺失，无法建立从 MCU 使能信号到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的完整路径。现有证据仅能确认路径的起点（MCU 使能引脚和信号）和终点（PHY 的电源引脚）。 **缺失信息：** 缺少使能信号经过的中间器件（如电源管理 IC、LDO）及其与 PHY 电源网络 `N22956687` 的电气连接关系。
